            [32m'Git Safety Protocol:\n'[39m +
            [32m'- NEVER update the git config\n'[39m +
            [32m"- NEVER run destructive git commands (push --force, reset --hard, checkout ., restore ., clean -f, branch -D) unless the user explicitly requests these actions. Taking unauthorized destructive actions is unhelpful and can result in lost work, so it's best to ONLY run these commands when given direct instructions \n"[39m +
            [32m'- NEVER skip hooks (--no-verify, --no-gpg-sign, etc) unless the user explicitly requests it\n'[39m +
            [32m'- NEVER run force push to main/master, warn the user if they request it\n'[39m +
            [32m'- CRITICAL: Always create NEW commits rather than amending, unless the user explicitly requests a git amend. When a pre-commit hook fails, the commit did NOT happen  — so --amend would modify the PREVIOUS commit, which may result in destroying work or losing previous changes. Instead, after hook failure, fix the issue, re-stage, and create a NEW commit\n'[39m +
            [32m'- When staging files, prefer adding specific files by name rather than using "git add -A" or "git add .", which can accidentally include sensitive files (.env, credentials) or large binaries\n'[39m +
            [32m'- NEVER commit changes unless the user explicitly asks you to. It is VERY IMPORTANT to only commit when explicitly asked, otherwise the user will feel that you are being too proactive\n'[39m +
            [32m'\n'[39m +
            [32m'1. You can call multiple tools in a single response. When multiple independent pieces of information are requested and all commands are likely to succeed, run multiple tool calls in parallel for optimal performance. run the following bash commands in parallel, each using the executeCommand tool:\n'[39m +
            [32m'  - Run a git status command to see all untracked files. IMPORTANT: Never use the -uall flag as it can cause memory issues on large repos.\n'[39m +
            [32m'  - Run a git diff command to see both staged and unstaged changes that will be committed.\n'[39m +
            [32m"  - Run a git log command to see recent commit messages, so that you can follow this repository's commit message style.\n"[39m +
            [32m'2. Analyze all staged changes (both previously staged and newly added) and draft a commit message:\n'[39m +
            [32m'  - Summarize the nature of the changes (eg. new feature, enhancement to an existing feature, bug fix, refactoring, test, docs, etc.). Ensure the message accurately reflects the changes and their purpose (i.e. "add" means a wholly new feature, "update" means an enhancement to an existing feature, "fix" means a bug fix, etc.).\n'[39m +
            [32m'  - Do not commit files that likely contain secrets (.env, credentials.json, etc). Warn the user if they specifically request to commit those files\n'[39m +
            [32m'  - Draft a concise (1-2 sentences) commit message that focuses on the "why" rather than the "what"\n'[39m +
            [32m'  - Ensure it accurately reflects the changes and their purpose\n'[39m +
            [32m'3. You can call multiple tools in a single response. When multiple independent pieces of information are requested and all commands are likely to succeed, run multiple tool calls in parallel for optimal performance. run the following commands:\n'[39m +

            [32m'\n'[39m +
            [32m'   Co-Authored-By: Pochi <noreply@getpochi.com>\n'[39m +
            [32m'\n'[39m +

            [32m'4. If the commit fails due to pre-commit hook changes, retry the commit ONCE to include these automated changes.             [32m'\n'[39m +

              text: [32m'# Real-Time Notification Center with Preferences in Wasp.sh\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'## Background\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'In modern web applications, real-time notifications are essential for keeping users engaged and informed. However, users must also have granular control over what notifications they receive to prevent alert fatigue. Wasp.sh (v0.24.0) provides built-in support for Socket.IO-based WebSockets alongside its full-stack Query/Action architecture. In this task, you will build a robust, real-time notification center that respects user preferences and supports batch status updates.\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'## Requirements\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'### 1. Database Schema (schema.prisma)\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'Define the following entities in your schema.prisma file:\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'* User entity:\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * Must have an autoincrementing integer id as primary key.\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * Must have a one-to-many relation to Notification.\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * Must have a one-to-one relation to NotificationPreference.\n'[39m +
                [32m'* Notification entity:\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * id: Autoincrementing integer primary key.\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * userId: Integer, foreign key to User.\n'[39m +
                [32m"  * type: String (one of 'SYSTEM', 'SECURITY', 'ACTIVITY').\n"[39m +
                [32m'  * title: String.\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * message: String.\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * isRead: Boolean, defaults to false.\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * createdAt: DateTime, defaults to now().\n'[39m +
                [32m'* NotificationPreference entity:\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * id: Autoincrementing integer primary key.\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * userId: Integer, unique, foreign key to User.\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * systemEnabled: Boolean, defaults to true.\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * securityEnabled: Boolean, defaults to true.\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * activityEnabled: Boolean, defaults to true.\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'### 2. Wasp Configuration (main.wasp.ts)\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'Configure the Wasp application spec with the following:\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'* Authentication: Enable usernameAndPassword auth, using User as the user entity, and redirecting failed authentication to /login.\n'[39m +
                [32m'* WebSockets: Enable WebSocket support with autoConnect: true, referencing a custom server-side function webSocketFn in src/webSocket.ts.\n'[39m +
                [32m'* Routes & Pages:\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * / -> MainPage (requires authentication).\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * /login -> LoginPage (public).\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * /signup -> SignupPage (public).\n'[39m +
                [32m'* Operations:\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * Query getNotifications (uses Notification entity).\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * Action batchUpdateNotificationStatus (uses Notification entity).\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * Query getNotificationPreferences (uses NotificationPreference entity).\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * Action updateNotificationPreferences (uses NotificationPreference and User entities).\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * Action triggerNotificationEvent (uses Notification, NotificationPreference, and User entities).\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'### 3. Server-Side Operations & WebSocket Integration\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +
                [32m'* WebSocket Setup (src/webSocket.ts):\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * Implement webSocketFn to initialize the Socket.IO server.\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * When a user connects, if they are authenticated, they should automatically join a Socket.IO room scoped to their user ID (e.g., user-${userId}).\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * Critical Seam: To allow server-side operations (like Actions) to emit WebSocket events, you must store a reference to the io server instance globally/module-level and export a helper/getter to retrieve it.\n'[39m +
                [32m'* Query getNotifications:\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * Returns all notifications for the authenticated user, ordered by createdAt descending.\n'[39m +
                [32m'* Action batchUpdateNotificationStatus:\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * Accepts { ids: number\\[], isRead: boolean }.\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * Updates the isRead status of all specified notifications belonging to the authenticated user, and returns the count of updated records.\n'[39m +
                [32m'* Query getNotificationPreferences:\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * Returns the NotificationPreference for the authenticated user.\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * If no preference record exists yet, the server must automatically create one with all preferences (systemEnabled, securityEnabled, activityEnabled) set to true and return it.\n'[39m +
                [32m'* Action updateNotificationPreferences:\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * Accepts { systemEnabled: boolean, securityEnabled: boolean, activityEnabled: boolean }.\n'[39m +
                [32m"  * Updates and returns the user's preferences.\n"[39m +
                [32m'* Action triggerNotificationEvent:\n'[39m +
                [32m"  * Accepts { type: 'SYSTEM' | 'SECURITY' | 'ACTIVITY', title: string, message: string }.\n"[39m +
                [32m"  * Checks the authenticated user's preferences. If the preference for the given type is enabled:\n"[39m +
                [32m'    * Creates a new Notification in the database.\n'[39m +
                [32m"    * Emits a real-time 'notification' event to the user's Socket.IO room/socket with the newly created Notification object as payload.\n"[39m +
                [32m'    * Returns { success: true, created: true }.\n'[39m +
                [32m'  * If the preference for the given type is disabled:\n'[39m +
                [32m'    * Does NOT create a notification in the database.\n'[39m +
                [32m'    * Does NOT emit any WebSocket event.\n'[39m +
                [32m'    * Returns { success: true, created: false }.\n'[39m +
                [32m'\n'[39m +

                [32m'    * Input field for title: data-testid="trigger-title"\n'[39m +
                [32m'    * Input field for message: data-testid="trigger-message"\n'[39m +

                  [32m'    },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  return { count: result.count };\n'[39m +
                  [32m'};\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'export const getNotificationPreferences = async (args: any, context: any) => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  if (!context.user) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    throw new HttpError(401, "User not authenticated.");\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const { NotificationPreference } = context.entities;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  let preference = await NotificationPreference.findUnique({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    where: {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      userId: context.user.id,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  if (!preference) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    preference = await NotificationPreference.create({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      data: {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        userId: context.user.id,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        systemEnabled: true,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        securityEnabled: true,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        activityEnabled: true,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  return preference;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'};\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'export const updateNotificationPreferences = async (\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  args: { systemEnabled: boolean; securityEnabled: boolean; activityEnabled: boolean },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  context: any\n'[39m +
                  [32m') => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  if (!context.user) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    throw new HttpError(401, "User not authenticated.");\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const { NotificationPreference } = context.entities;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  return await NotificationPreference.upsert({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    where: {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      userId: context.user.id,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    update: {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      systemEnabled: args.systemEnabled,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      securityEnabled: args.securityEnabled,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      activityEnabled: args.activityEnabled,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    create: {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      userId: context.user.id,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      systemEnabled: args.systemEnabled,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      securityEnabled: args.securityEnabled,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      activityEnabled: args.activityEnabled,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'};\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'export const triggerNotificationEvent = async (\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  args: { type: "SYSTEM" | "SECURITY" | "ACTIVITY"; title: string; message: string },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  context: any\n'[39m +
                  [32m') => {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  if (!context.user) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    throw new HttpError(401, "User not authenticated.");\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  const { Notification, NotificationPreference } = context.entities;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  let preference = await NotificationPreference.findUnique({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    where: {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      userId: context.user.id,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  if (!preference) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    preference = await NotificationPreference.create({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      data: {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        userId: context.user.id,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        systemEnabled: true,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        securityEnabled: true,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        activityEnabled: true,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  let isEnabled = false;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  if (args.type === "SYSTEM") {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    isEnabled = preference.systemEnabled;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  } else if (args.type === "SECURITY") {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    isEnabled = preference.securityEnabled;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  } else if (args.type === "ACTIVITY") {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    isEnabled = preference.activityEnabled;\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  if (isEnabled) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    const notification = await Notification.create({\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      data: {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        userId: context.user.id,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        type: args.type,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        title: args.title,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        message: args.message,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        isRead: false,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      },\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    });\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    const io = getIoInstance();\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    if (io) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      io.to(`user-${context.user.id}`).emit("notification", notification);\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      console.log(`Emitted notification event to user-${context.user.id}`);\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    } else {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      console.warn("Socket.IO server instance not initialized yet!");\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    return { success: true, created: true };\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'  return { success: true, created: false };\n'[39m +
                  [32m'};\n'[39m +
                  [32m''[39m

            [32m'- When staging files, prefer adding specific files by name rather than using "git add -A" or "git add .", which can accidentally include sensitive files (.env, credentials) or large binaries\n'[39m +
